# FR — France sights: individual worldwide reviews

- [Worldwide v2](../../../rules.md) · [Workflow](../../future-country-workflow.md)
- Initial editorial inventory: 56 UNESCO inscriptions and 31 tentative nominations at property level, all 11 national parks (Vanoise, Écrins and Port-Cros overlap tentative nominations). Overseas properties included under FR because separate territory codes are not registered in this repository. Other serial components selectively represented. All GPS approximate, especially dispersed properties; verify each before navigation. Current peer YAML inspected; broad type group comparisons are provisional and narrower same-type peers needed. No full independent material-claim verification or new perfect-ten anchors.
